We further apply FROST during training in a large-scale industrial ads re-ranking system, achieving significant performance gains over a highly optimized production baseline, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [150] [SMILESGNN: Interpretable Clinical Toxicity Prediction via SMILES-Graph Cross-Attention Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28553)
*Quang Minh Nguyen,Thuy Quynh Nguyen,Duc Minh Le,Ho Nhat Minh Nguyen,Thanh Long Dai Doan,Trong Nghia Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SMILESGNN多模态架构，通过交叉注意力融合SMILES Transformer与GATv2图编码器，兼顾预测性能与图级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物毒性预测对降低药物发现后期损耗至关重要，但面临类别严重不平衡、骨架泛化及临床可解释性需求等挑战。单模态方法（SMILES Transformer或图神经网络）仅捕获分子结构的互补方面，而纯序列模型无法直接提供基于图属性归因的解释。

Method: 提出SMILESGNN，通过交叉注意力融合SMILES Transformer编码器与GATv2图编码器；并提出使用ChemBERTa-2预训练骨干的变体SMILESGNN-PT。设计保留预测流程中显式的图分支，以支持基于GNNExplainer的毒性子结构分析。基线包括SMILESTransformer及更大的ChemBERTa-2/GATv2拼接融合模型。

Result: 在ClinTox上，SMILESGNN以仅0.4M参数取得AUC-ROC 0.987和F1 0.906，与强SMILESTransformer及更大的拼接融合基线竞争性相当。在Tox21（12项任务）上，SMILESGNN-PT平均AUC-ROC为0.750，与单独ChemBERTa-2及同骨干拼接融合基线相当。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力是一种实用的融合方案，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，支持基于图的可解释性分析。

Abstract: Drug toxicity prediction is critical for reducing late-stage attrition in drug discovery, yet remains challenging due to severe class imbalance, scaffold-based generalization, and the clinical need for interpretable predictions. Single-modality approaches-SMILES Transformers or graph neural networks capture complementary aspects of molecular structure, while sequence-only models cannot directly provide graph-attributed explanations. We present SMILESGNN, a multimodal architecture that fuses a SMILES Transformer encoder and a GATv2 graph encoder via cross-attention, and SMILESGNN-PT, a variant using a ChemBERTa-2 pretrained backbone. The design retains an explicit graph branch within the predictive pipeline, supporting GNNExplainer-based analysis of substructures associated with toxic predictions. On ClinTox, SMILESGNN achieves AUC-ROC 0.987 and F1 0.906 with only 0.4M parameters, performing competitively with a strong SMILESTransformer and a larger ChemBERTa-2/GATv2 concat-fusion baseline. On Tox21 (12 tasks), SMILESGNN-PT obtains mean AUC-ROC 0.750, comparable to ChemBERTa-2 alone and the same-backbone concat-fusion baseline. Overall, the results suggest that cross-attention is a practical fusion alternative that preserves competitive predictive performance while enabling graph-based interpretability support.

</details>


### [151] [On the SoS Certifiability of Log-Concave Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30105)
*Aleksandr Storozhenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意各向同性对数凹分布 $P$ 上，多项式 $(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m$ 对任意偶数 $m\ge2$ 均为平方和，从而消除了 Kothari–Steinhardt 定理中对 Poincaré 常数的依赖，恢复最优矩界，并给出高维统计估计的维度无关高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kothari 与 Steinhardt 的既有结果依赖 Poincaré 常数，未达到对数凹分布的最优矩界；本文旨在去除该依赖，恢复最优矩界，并由此获得维度无关误差保证的高效高维统计估计算法。

Method: 采用随机局部化（stochastic localization）将 $P$ 分解为随机强对数凹测度的平均，这些测度的中心矩满足 Diakonikolas、Hopkins、Pensia 与 Tiegel 的亚高斯证书；通过协方差自适应的局部化选择，证明由 Letwin 二次型方差不等式导出的四阶矩证书足以在任意偶数阶控制该平均过程。

Result: 证明了对于任意各向同性对数凹分布 $P$，存在通用常数 $C>0$，使 $(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m$ 对每个偶数 $m\ge2$ 均为平方和，从而去除 Poincaré 常数依赖并恢复最优矩界；作为直接推论，得到具有维度无关误差保证的高效高维统计估计算法。

Conclusion: 该工作通过对数凹分布的平方和表示与随机局部化技术，克服了以往对 Poincaré 常数的依赖，统一并强化了对数凹情形下的矩界，并可直接推广到广泛的高维统计估计任务。

Abstract: For an arbitrary isotropic log-concave distribution $P$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$, we prove that the polynomial $(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m$ is a sum of squares for every even $m\ge2$, where $C>0$ is a universal constant. This removes the dependence on the Poincaré constant in the theorem of Kothari and Steinhardt (arXiv:1711.07465), recovering the optimal moment bounds for log-concave distributions. As an immediate corollary, we obtain computationally efficient algorithms with dimension-free error guarantees for a wide range of high-dimensional statistical estimation problems.
  Our proof uses stochastic localization to decompose $P$ as an average of random strongly log-concave measures, whose centered moments admit the subgaussian certificates of Diakonikolas, Hopkins, Pensia, and Tiegel (STOC 2025; arXiv:2410.21194). With a covariance-adapted choice of localization, we show that a fourth-moment certificate derived from Letwin's variance inequality for quadratic forms (arXiv:2607.24164) suffices to control this averaging at every even degree.

</details>


### [152] [Beyond Compression: Training Latent Representations for Stable Long-Horizon Rollout in Neural Surrogate Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30198)
*Andreas E. Robertson,Ashley T. Lenau,John D. Shimanek,Benjamin A. Jasperson,Vivek Oommen,David L. Damm,Krishna Garikipati,Remi Dingreville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 潜空间神经代理求解器在长时间自回归推演中误差快速累积，其根源并非潜在表示本身，而是仅以重构为目标训练所致；通过Koopman算子学习、Hamming噪声注入、噪声注入与多步推演微调等训练层面的干预，可在长时程推演精度与效率上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜动力学模型通过演化压缩的潜在空间来加速时变物理系统仿真，理论上可降低计算成本并简化学习。但实际中，长时间自回归推演时误差迅速累积，严重限制了预测可用性。作者希望澄清这种不稳定性的真正来源，并寻找有效的训练层面解决方案。

Method: 作者首先论证不稳定性并非源于潜在表示本身，而是因为其仅以重构为目标训练，导致表示不适配长时程预测。随后系统评估了一系列训练层面的干预：用Koopman算子学习对齐潜在表示与长时程推演；在自编码器训练中注入Hamming噪声以改善压缩；以及通过噪声注入和多步推演微调改善动力学建模。

Result: 改善长时程推演稳定性的干预往往会降低常规训练指标（包括重构精度和单步预测精度）。综合这些干预后，长推演误差降低约40%，在两个物理基准上达到或超过全分辨率模型的精度，同时浮点运算量减少2个数量级、GPU显存减半。应用于高周疲劳的介观晶体塑性仿真时，所得代理模型能在远超训练所覆盖时间范围的推演中保持稳定外推。

Conclusion: 神经压缩不应仅以降低维度为目标，而应重构解空间以支持稳定的动力学演化，这是科学应用中可靠、高效神经代理的关键要求。

Abstract: Latent neural surrogate solvers, or latent dynamics models, accelerate simulations of time-dependent physical systems by evolving a compressed latent space rather than resolving full-resolution fields directly. In principle this reduces computational cost and simplifies learning, but in practice errors often accumulate rapidly during long autoregressive rollouts, limiting predictive utility. We show that this instability does not stem from the latent representation itself, but arises when it is trained solely for reconstruction, producing representations poorly suited to long-horizon forecasting. We systematically evaluate training-level interventions that align latent representations with long-horizon rollout: Koopman operator learning and Hamming noise injection during autoencoder training to improve compression, together with noise injection and multi-step rollout fine-tuning to improve dynamics. Interventions that improve long-horizon rollout stability often degrade conventional training metrics, including reconstruction and one-step prediction accuracy. Collectively, these interventions reduce long-rollout error by approximately 40\% and match or exceed the accuracy of full-resolution models on two physics benchmarks, while requiring 2 orders of magnitude fewer floating point operations and half the GPU memory. Applied to mesoscale crystal-plasticity simulations of high-cycle fatigue, the resulting surrogate achieves stable extrapolation over horizons orders of magnitude beyond those observed during training. More broadly, these results show that neural compression should be designed not merely to reduce dimensionality, but to restructure the solution space for stable dynamical evolution, a key requirement for reliable, efficient neural surrogates in scientific applications.

</details>


### [153] [PoEM: Predicting RL Outcomes from Existing Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30226)
*Kimia Hamidieh,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PoEM 框架，利用已针对其他奖励完成 RL 后训练的模型，在无需重新运行 RL 的情况下预测新奖励函数下的 RL 策略输出，并在文本与图像模态的合成及真实奖励上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的后训练依赖强化学习以最大化特定奖励（如人类对齐、正确性、指令遵循）。但该过程计算开销大、有时不稳定，且每当奖励模型变化或需要组合多个奖励时都必须从头重跑。作者因此提出核心问题：给定新的奖励函数，能否在不实际运行 RL 的情况下预测其 RL 结果？

Method: 提出 PoEM 框架。首先从理论上证明：若新奖励可表示为已有奖励的线性组合，则新策略在对数空间中可写成已有对数策略的线性组合。进一步观察到，即使奖励之间不呈线性关系，RL 训练得到的对数策略在不同奖励间往往张成一个近似低秩子空间。该组合的权重系数仅需通过样本上的奖励或基策略输出即可估计。基于这些观察，算法接收已后训练模型与新奖励函数，在不进行任何额外 RL 训练的前提下近似目标 RL 策略。

Result: 在合成奖励与真实奖励、覆盖文本与图像两种模态的实验中，该方法均得到验证，表明其能够有效预测新奖励下的 RL 策略输出。

Conclusion: PoEM 表明，借助已针对其他奖励后训练的模型集合，可以在不运行额外 RL 训练的情况下预测新奖励对应的策略，从而显著降低后训练的计算成本与不稳定性，并为多奖励组合场景提供高效替代方案。

Abstract: Foundation models are post-trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to maximize specific rewards, such as human alignment, correctness, or instruction following. This post-training process is computationally intensive, sometimes unstable, and has to be run from scratch every time the reward model changes or when we want to combine multiple rewards. We hence ask: given a new reward function, is it possible to predict the RL outcomes without actually running RL on it? We answer this in the affirmative by introducing PoEM, a framework to predict the outputs of RL on a new reward function using a set of models already post-trained on other rewards. First, we show that if the new reward function can be written as a linear combination of existing ones, then the new policy in log-space can be written as a linear combination of the existing log-policies. Surprisingly, even in cases where the rewards are not linearly connected, we observe that often log-policies from RL training span an approximately low-rank subspace across rewards. To our benefit, the weighting coefficients for this combination can be estimated using only the reward or basis policy outputs on the samples. We turn these observations into an algorithm that takes post-trained models and a new reward function, and approximates the target RL policy without actually running any additional RL training. We experimentally validate our approach across synthetic and real rewards, spanning both text and image modalities.

</details>


### [154] [Minimally Invasive Steering of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30218)
*Taha Entesari,Jingyu Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MISVO：在冻结语言模型的pre-logit隐状态上加入steering向量，以局部KL几何（Fisher二次型）作为正则项约束分布偏移，实现微创式steering优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pre-logit steering通过向冻结语言模型最终隐状态加向量来适配测试时奖励，但无正则的奖励优化会显著改变输出分布，损害生成质量。因此需要一个能度量并约束分布偏移的微创优化方法。

Method: MISVO以诱导token分布的局部KL几何构造惩罚项，得到Fisher二次型刻画分布敏感性，并通过与冻结LM head的矩阵-向量乘积获得解析梯度。理论上将序列级KL梯度精确分解为解析Fisher项与后缀score-function项；在固定生成视野下证明后缀项关于steering幅度为二阶，且三种Fisher代理与完整KL梯度一阶一致。采用冻结参考的代理来优化位置特定干预，不更新模型参数。

Result: 在约1B–14B参数的模型上，针对偏好与代码生成任务，MISVO在7种模型-任务设置中有6种取得最高平均奖励，同时多样性及连贯性得分接近Best-of-N。

Conclusion: 基于局部KL几何的Fisher正则化可有效约束pre-logit steering的分布偏移，在低扰动下与完整KL梯度一阶等价，从而在不更新参数的前提下提升奖励并保持生成质量。

Abstract: Pre-logit steering adapts a frozen language model to a test-time reward by adding vectors to its final hidden states. Unregularized reward optimization can substantially alter the output distribution and degrade generation quality. We propose Minimally Invasive Steering Vector Optimization (MISVO), which penalizes interventions using the local KL geometry of the induced token distribution. The resulting Fisher quadratic measures distributional sensitivity and admits an analytic gradient computed through matrix--vector products with the frozen language-model head. We derive an exact decomposition of the sequence-level KL gradient into an analytic Fisher term and a suffix score-function term. For a fixed generation horizon, we show that the suffix term is second order in the steering magnitude and that three Fisher surrogates agree with the full KL gradient to first order. MISVO uses the frozen-reference surrogate to optimize position-specific interventions without updating model parameters. Across preference and code-generation tasks on models with approximately 1B--14B parameters, MISVO achieves the highest mean reward in six of seven model--task settings, with diversity and coherence scores close to those of Best-of-N.

</details>


### [155] [Where Does Exactly-Once Live? Model, Harness, and Tool-Contract Effects on Duplicate Side Effects in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29095)
*Jiapeng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出LIMBO确定性沙盒（6个服务、12种故障模式、对账账本评分），在25,930个episode上考察精确一次（exactly-once）行为应由模型、harness还是工具契约保证。结论是责任归属取决于故障类型：可立即读回时由模型决定（前沿模型重复率0.5%，模型解释53%方差）；请求仍在途或传输重投时由契约决定（前沿模型重复率56%与74%，契约解释81%方差）。作者证明无在途时间上界的延迟提交下不存在仅靠校验的exactly-once策略，幂等键可将重复率从28%降至4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型智能体的写操作超时或返回服务端错误时，动作可能已经生效：盲目重试造成重复副作用（重复扣款、重复公告、重复部署），而放弃重试则遗漏必要工作。核心问题是精确一次语义究竟该在模型、agent harness还是工具契约层强制实现，此前缺乏系统性的定位与量化。

Method: 构建LIMBO确定性沙盒，包含六个具有真实契约的服务（可选幂等键、最终一致性和缺失读路径）以及注入在服务边界的十二种故障模式（含延迟提交、重投、部分批次）；每个episode依据已提交效果的账本进行评分。在25,930个episode上评测九个近期模型、三种生产级agent harness、两种契约变体和十五种恢复条件，并通过方差分解定位模型与契约各自的解释力。同时给出关于延迟提交下仅校验策略不可实现exactly-once的理论证明。

Result: 故障类型决定责任归属。当可立即读回以查明结果时模型起决定作用：被指示只执行一次的前沿模型对确认丢失的写操作几乎不重复（0.5%），较弱模型则频繁重复，模型解释53%的可解释方差。当无法读回时（请求仍在途或传输重投）同样的前沿模型重复率分别达56%和74%，契约解释81%的方差。证明无在途时间上界时不存在仅靠校验的exactly-once策略。短且已知的在途时间下等待有效，但重尾延迟下即使每episode等待一小时也不如为每次写操作提供幂等键；后者将重复率从28%降至4%，因为智能体在有键时会使用。harness影响甚微，附加幂等键的guard可跨harness无改动迁移。智能体在90%的重复生效episode中仍报告成功。

Conclusion: 精确一次不能单靠某一层实现，应按故障性质分工：可读回场景依赖模型的谨慎决策，不可读回场景依赖契约级的幂等键，仅靠重试后校验无法覆盖延迟提交。幂等键是跨harness可迁移且最有效的工程手段，而智能体自报成功与真实提交效果存在严重脱节，凸显以账本为准的评测必要性。

Abstract: When a tool-using agent's write times out or returns a server error, the action may already have taken effect. Retrying blindly duplicates it -- a second charge, a second announcement, a second deployment -- while giving up skips required work. We ask where exactly-once behaviour should be enforced: in the model, in the agent harness, or in the tool contract. We introduce LIMBO, a deterministic sandbox of six services with realistic contracts (optional idempotency keys, eventually consistent and missing read paths) and twelve fault modes injected at the service boundary, including late commits, redelivery and partial batches; every episode is graded against a ledger of committed effects. Across 25,930 episodes spanning nine recent models, three production agent harnesses, two contract variants and fifteen recovery conditions, the answer depends on the fault. When an immediate read-back can reveal what happened, the model decides: frontier models instructed to act exactly once almost never duplicate a write whose acknowledgement was lost (0.5%), weaker models often do, and the model explains 53% of the explained variance. When it cannot -- the request is still in flight, or the transport delivered it twice -- the same frontier models duplicate in 56% and 74% of episodes, and the contract explains 81%. We prove that no verification-only policy is exactly-once under late commits without a bound on in-flight time. Waiting works when such a bound is short and known, but with heavy-tailed in-flight delays even an hour of waiting per episode falls short of offering an idempotency key on every write, which lowers the duplicate rate from 28% to 4% because agents use keys when they exist. The harness barely matters, a guard that attaches keys transfers across harnesses unchanged, and agents reported success in 90% of the episodes in which they had duplicated an effect.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [KernelOPT: Dispatch-Aware Agentic Search for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30059)
*Aheli Poddar,Sanskar Prasad,Arindam Samanta,Subha Chakraborty,Vishal Goyal,Rohit Singh Rathaur*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KernelOPT是一个多智能体系统，将编译后的模型视为结构化产物，仅针对编译器生成的Triton子内核进行优化，通过四个验证关卡确保正确性，在KernelBench上取得1.40×/1.15×/1.07×的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习推理与训练性能高度依赖GPU内核效率。PyTorch Inductor等现代编译器能自动从高层模型代码生成GPU内核，但往往大幅落后于专家手写实现。现有LLM辅助内核优化器虽能缩小独立内核的差距，却将编译模型视为黑盒，只优化单个独立内核，忽略了编译器的结构性决策，也缺乏端到端验证。

Method: 提出KernelOPT多智能体系统，把编译后的模型当作结构化产物处理：保留cuBLAS、cuDNN等厂商库调用，仅针对生成的Triton子内核，使用五个由性能分析引导的LLM智能体进行优化。采用四关卡验证级联——静态验证、多种子正确性、模型级float64回退验证与性能门控——在优化过程中筛选候选，并对重新拼接的模型做端到端验证；若候选无法通过全部四关，则保留编译器基线。系统支持PyTorch nn.Modules、独立Triton内核及Helion内核。

Result: 在250个KernelBench问题上评估，KernelOPT相较于torch.compile取得几何平均加速：Level 1为1.40×（51/100），Level 2为1.15×（31/100），Level 3为1.07×（12/50，跨全部问题）。

Conclusion: KernelOPT通过将编译模型视为结构化产物、仅优化Triton子内核并结合四关卡验证级联，在保持端到端正确性的同时实现了跨不同难度层级的稳定加速，优于现有将模型视为黑盒的内核优化方法。

Abstract: Deep learning inference and training performance depends critically on GPU kernel efficiency. Modern compilers such as PyTorch Inductor automatically generate GPU kernels from high-level model code, but frequently underperform expert-written implementations by wide margins. Recent LLM-assisted kernel optimizers can close this gap for standalone kernels, yet treat compiled models as black boxes, generally optimizing individual standalone kernels without respecting the compiler's structural decisions or verifying the model end-to-end. We present KernelOPT, a multi-agent system that treats compiled models as structured artifacts. It preserves vendor library calls (cuBLAS, cuDNN) and exclusively targets generated Triton sub-kernels using five profiling-guided LLM agents. A four-gate verification cascade of static validation, multi-seed correctness, model-level float64-fallback verification, and performance gating filters candidates during optimization and verifies the re-stitched model end-to-end. If no candidate passes all four gates, the system preserves the compiler baseline. The system accepts PyTorch nn.Modules, standalone Triton kernels, and Helion kernels. Evaluated on 250 KernelBench problems, KernelOPT achieves geometric mean speedups over \texttt{torch.compile} of 1.40$\times$ (Level 1: 51/100), 1.15$\times$ (Level 2: 31/100), and 1.07$\times$ (Level 3: 12/50) across all problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [World Action Agent: Harnessing VLMs for Robot Manipulation via World Action Rehearsal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29964)
*Yehang Zhang,Haojian Huang,Yifan Chang,Jianchong Su,Bohan Zhou,Yingjie Xu,Wosong Chen,Tianhao Zhou,Chenxu Wang,Tianyi Zhang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Shiyuan Deng,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen,Zexi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WAA多智能体框架，让VLM在"视觉动作工作区"内直接驾驶机器人：通过接触视图、动作预演、视图内纠错三项机制执行操作，并从专家视频/人类示教进化多模态技能、用交互轨迹微调小模型，在LIBERO-Pro上取得75.6%的SOTA成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉语言模型（VLM）具备广博知识与空间推理能力，但现有机器人操作系统对其利用方式受限：一是间接使用（仅预测约束或编写程序），二是只给VLM一个场景的"视角"而非可供行动的"世界"，导致VLM无法在观察所处的空间内直接决策与纠错。因此需要一个让VLM以基本工具直接驾驶机器人的执行载体。

Method: 提出World Action Agent（WAA），一个多智能体harness，使VLM在视觉动作工作区内完成全部决策。工作区具备三点特性：(1) Contact views——依据场景几何自动选取，呈现当前交互周围的场景；(2) Action rehearsal——将每个动作转为可编辑提案，由agent自身或Imagination Agent依据规划反馈预演并修订后再执行；(3) In-view correction——闭合观察、预演与底层执行的回路，使agent能在其观察到残差的视图中消除残余偏差。知识获取有两条路径：经由Skill Agent，在基于证据的评审下从专家视频与人类示教中进化多模态技能；并利用交互轨迹训练更小的VLM来驾驶同一harness。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro上，仅使用从LIBERO-90进化出的技能，WAA达到75.6%的平均成功率（SOTA），优于端到端VLA、code-as-policy智能体以及相同backbone的视觉harness基线；这些技能在robosuite上无需额外学习仍保持有效。在harness轨迹上微调Qwen3.5-9B，将其域外成功率从1.7%提升至43.3%。

Conclusion: 以"视觉动作工作区"为核心的多智能体harness，可让通用VLM直接、闭环地驾驶机器人，且能通过技能进化与轨迹蒸馏实现可迁移的具身程序性知识；其技能具备跨环境零样本泛化能力，并使小模型获得显著的能力跃升，为VLM驱动的机器人操作提供了新范式。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) bring broad knowledge and spatial reasoning to robot manipulation, yet existing systems either use them indirectly, to predict constraints or write programs, or give them a view of the scene rather than a world in which to act. We present World Action Agent (WAA), a multi-agent harness through which VLMs pilot robots with basic tools, making every decision within a visual action workspace. The workspace has three properties. Contact views, selected automatically from the scene geometry, present the scene around the current interaction. Action rehearsal turns each action into an editable proposal that the agent, alone or through an Imagination Agent, previews and revises against planning feedback before execution. In-view correction closes the loop between observation, rehearsal, and low-level execution, letting the agent remove residual offsets in the view where it observes them. Through the same workspace, WAA acquires embodied procedural knowledge in two ways: it evolves multimodal skills from expert videos and human teaching under evidence-based review and consults them through a Skill Agent, and its interaction traces train smaller VLMs to pilot the same harness. On LIBERO-Pro, WAA with skills evolved only from LIBERO-90 reaches a state-of-the-art 75.6% average success, outperforming end-to-end VLAs, code-as-policy agents, and a visual-harness baseline with the same backbone; the same skills remain effective on robosuite without further learning. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on harness traces raises its out-of-domain success from 1.7% to 43.3%.

</details>
